# Spin-offs and demergers — NiftyTotalMarket

*Run 2026-10-08 · filings window 2025-10-08 → 2026-10-07 · 1023 restructuring filings across 256 companies · 76 spin-off situations, 245 other restructurings · judge: claude-opus-5 read the schemes and calls.*

**The method, in one paragraph.** Spin-offs are where the notes say the bargains hide: the new stock is sold by holders who never chose it (index funds, size and mandate limits, no coverage) for about a year, the parent is often the better buy before the split, insiders tell you what they think through the stock and options they take in the new entity, and a pro-forma statement plus the peer P/E prices what the market is ignoring. This report lists every spin-off, demerger and subsidiary listing announced or indicated in the last year — from the exchange filings and from what management said on the calls — walks each one's stage, and answers the checklist from the documents on file.

## HEGAM — HEG Advanced Materials Ltd.

**LISTED <1Y — selling-pressure window; the bargain forms here** · score 3.3/10 · spin-off · 23 filing(s) from BSE-screener, NSE · first 2026-01-09, latest 2026-09-29

**The stages, as filed:** announced (2026-01-13) → meetings (2026-04-02) → nclt_sanction (2026-08-18) → record_date (2026-08-24) → listed (2026-07-29)

**Stated in the filings:** resulting company **HEG Graphite Limited**; demerged undertaking: — i.e., the Company's graphite business — is to be demerged from HEG Limited into HEG Graphite; entitlement **1 for every 1**; appointed date 2024-04-01; size hint: 0% of ebitda.

**The judge's read of the documents:**

- *What is spun:* HEG's graphite electrodes business (including other carbon products) demerged into HEG Graphite Limited; parent retains thermal/hydel power plus amalgamated Bhilwara Energy.
- *Why (as stated):* Core businesses (graphite electrodes, thermal/hydel power) acquired critical mass and need flexibility, independence and focused management; segregation unlocks value, attracts different investors, matches different risk-return profiles and growth trajectories. — class `value_unlocking`
- *Insiders:* Promoter/Promoter Group holds 100% (50,000 shares) of HEG Graphite pre-Scheme, 56.28% post-Scheme on mirror basis; in parent HEG promoter stake rises 56.28% to 60.72% via BEL amalgamation. Other incentives not stated.
- *What it reveals:* Valuer puts the demerged Graphite Business at Rs 393.4/share versus Rs 124.0 for post-demerger HEG; the spin takes Rs 4,329.30 crore assets, Rs 3,810.32 crore net worth and all Rs 1,309.63 crore revenue.
- *Selling pressure:* unclear — Documents state no index treatment or listing mechanics; spin is large (1:1 mirror, entire graphite revenue), not a small stub.
- *Parent or spin:* spin · *option pricing date:* not stated
- *Read next:* PwC valuation report (Annexure J) and ICICI Securities fairness opinion (Annexure K) for graphite standalone basis.
- *In their words:* “Each of the said businesses required flexibility and independence to grow faster in the fast-changing technology and innovation driven environment.”

## NH — Narayana Hrudayalaya Ltd.

**PRE-SPIN — study the parent; the pieces do not trade yet** · score 5.7/10 · spin-off · 18 filing(s) from NSE · first 2025-12-12, latest 2026-04-06

- *What is spun:* Clinical Services undertaking of NH Integrated Care — 10 Bengaluru clinics — demerged into listed Narayana Hrudayalaya Ltd
- *Why (as stated):* Separation lets NHIC exclusively pursue the Aarogyam preventive healthcare business; transfer to NHL enables unified patient pathways, optimised specialists, consolidated admin functions and resource efficiency, ending duplicate functions — class `regulatory_strategic`
- *Insiders:* Not stated. Documents say only that there is no change in NHL's shareholding pattern and NHL continues to hold 100% of NHIC's equity capital
- *What it reveals:* Clinics are tiny — Rs 39.94 crore turnover, 1.11% of NHL standalone — and were dependent on hospital consultants, diagnostics and tertiary pathways while carrying duplicate admin costs; Aarogyam is retained as the asset-light growth platform
- *Selling pressure:* unlikely — No shares issued, no cash consideration, no new listing, no change in shareholding pattern; demerged division is 1.11% of turnover
- *Parent or spin:* parent · *option pricing date:* not stated
- *Read next:* The Meridian Medical Research & Hospital amalgamation scheme filed with NCLT Bengaluru on September 27, 2025
- *In their words:* “The Clinical Services undertaking of NHIC, comprising 10 clinics located in Bengaluru, shall be demerged to NHL.”

| Item | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|

| A hidden value revealed (pro-forma, leverage, comparables) | partly | borrowings 5,858 cr (Mar 2026) = 0.14× market cap; P/E 51.5 on Mar 2026 profit vs industry median 52.7 (45 peers) *(next: pro-forma P&L of each piece × the peer P/E of its own industry)* |

## HINDUNILVR — Hindustan Unilever Ltd.

**LISTED <1Y — selling-pressure window; the bargain forms here** · score 5.7/10 · spin-off · 13 filing(s) from NSE · first 2025-10-30, latest 2026-02-13

**The stages, as filed:** announced (2025-10-30) → listed (2025-11-29)

**Stated in the filings:** entitlement **1 for every 1**; record date 2025-12-05; appointed date 2025-12-01.

**The judge's read of the documents:**

- *What is spun:* HUL's Ice Cream Business Undertaking (Kwality Wall's) demerged into Kwality Wall's (India) Limited, a pure-play ice cream company
- *Why (as stated):* Separation beneficial for both companies: tailoring the operating model to ice cream's cold chain, and focusing the investment algorithm and incentives on ice cream — class `value_unlocking`
- *Insiders:* Promoter stake not stated; HUL holders get 1 KWIL share per HUL share. HUL CFO Ritesh Tiwari non-executive director; Prashant Premrajka executive director/CFO; Chitrank Goel deputy MD
- *What it reveals:* A #2 player in a market compounding ~10-11% is leaving at depressed economics: FY24-25 EBITDA margin 7.1%, H1 FY25-26 0.0%, against top-tier gross margins and a cost gap to global ice cream benchmarks — suppressed earnings, not a bad business
- *Selling pressure:* likely — Small, low-margin unit (HUL margin improves 50-60 bps without it), different cold-chain industry, index holders receive unwanted 1:1 shares
- *Parent or spin:* spin · *option pricing date:* not stated
- *Read next:* KWIL Information Memorandum — Risk Factors and standalone financials explaining H1 FY25-26 EBITDA falling to 0.0%
- *In their words:* “Ice Cream demerger is expected to result in an improvement of 50-60 bps to the reported margin as Ice Cream business operates at a margin lower than HUL average.”

## How this was built

- **Filings:** NSE's corporate-announcements feed read month by month for the 2025-10-08 → 2026-10-07 window and filtered to the 750 official NiftyTotalMarket constituents; BSE via its daily RSS (accumulating) and the BSE-sourced per-company lists on screener.in (BSE's own API refuses this environment). Each filing is classified by category and headline; its attachment is reduced to text and re-classified from the text, so a bare 'Scheme of Arrangement' becomes a demerger or a merger once the scheme is read. Stored in `IndividualStockAnalysis/India/Announcements/NiftyTotalMarket/`.
- **Situations:** one per company per kind family; stages from the filings' words; facts (entitlement ratio, resulting company, dates, pro-forma, option pricing, insider continuity, size) by pattern from the texts; reasons classified into the notes' five.
- **Calls:** the stored consolidated transcripts, last 12 months of calls, scanned for demerger / spin-off / listing language with verbatim snippets.
- **Numbers:** borrowings from the stored balance sheets, net profit from the stored P&L, market cap / P/E from the live Nifty 500 market file (stated date), the industry median P/E from the same file. `stub_value()` and `leverage_doubling()` carry the notes' partial-spin-off and Host Marriott arithmetic for when the pieces trade.
- **Judge:** claude-opus-5 read the schemes and calls. The judge reads only the documents shown to it and must quote; its read is cached per document hash and model.
- **Score:** share of checklist items answered yes (1) or partly (½), on 10. It measures how much of the notes' pattern is VISIBLE in the documents, not how good the stock is.
